Accessories can be:
- **Held** (left/right side)
- **Worn** (on head, body)
- **Attached** (to back, feet)
- **Environmental** (around character)
- **Combined** (multiple at once)

All maintain minimalist design while adding functional context!

**assistant** (Jul 13, 2025, 10:55 PM)

 Based on your questions, I can see you're building towards an ASCII animation system for a character-driven adventure/game - something with the charm and personality of Wallace & Gromit but using minimal ASCII constraints. Here's what you'll need:

```
                    FLATBOY ANIMATION SYSTEM SPEC
                    ═══════════════════════════════

CORE ANIMATION FRAMEWORK:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

FRAME TIMING SYSTEM (30fps equivalent):
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ IDLE: 4-frame loop @ 8fps                              │
│ WALK: 6-frame cycle @ 12fps                            │
│ RUN:  4-frame cycle @ 24fps                            │
│ JUMP: 8-frame sequence (no loop)                       │
│ EMOTE: 2-6 frames contextual                           │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

PERSONALITY ARCHETYPES:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

NERVOUS TYPE          CONFIDENT TYPE        SLEEPY TYPE          EXCITABLE TYPE
┌──────────┐         ┌──────────┐         ┌──────────┐         ┌──────────┐
│Idle:     │         │Idle:     │         │Idle:     │         │Idle:     │
│ ⌣  ⌣    │         │ ●  ●    │         │ ◑  ◑    │         │ ◕  ◕    │
│   ⌢      │         │   ⌢      │         │   ︶      │         │   ◡      │
│*fidgets* │         │*steady*  │         │*sways*   │         │*bounces* │
└──────────┘         └──────────┘         └──────────┘         └──────────┘

Each personality has unique:
- Idle animations (breathing, blinking, fidgeting)
- Walk cycles (shuffling vs striding vs bouncing)
- Reaction timing (instant vs delayed vs gradual)
- Emote preferences (which expressions they favor)

MICRO-EXPRESSIONS SYSTEM:

CUTSCENE CHOREOGRAPHY:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

[SCENE: Morning Routine]
FRAME 1-4: Wake up (sleepy → alert)
FRAME 5-8: Stretch animation
FRAME 9-12: Notice coffee → excitement build
FRAME 13-16: Quick dash to coffee
FRAME 17-20: Satisfied sip → content expression

TECHNICAL REQUIREMENTS:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

1. SPRITE SHEETS (7x10 character grid per frame)
2. TRANSITION RULES (which animations can chain)
3. EMOTION STATE MACHINE (tracks mood over time)
4. PROP ATTACHMENT POINTS (for accessories)
5. COLLISION BOXES (simplified 3x3 grid)
6. SOUND CUE TRIGGERS (per animation frame)

STORY-DRIVEN ELEMENTS YOU'LL NEED:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

CHARACTER RELATIONSHIPS:
- Friendship meters affect idle animations when near
- Shared emotes (synchronized reactions)
- Character-specific interaction animations

NARRATIVE MOMENTS:
- Signature moves (each character's unique animations)
- Dramatic pauses (held frames with subtle movement)
- Comedy timing (delayed reactions, double-takes)

WORLD INTERACTION:
- Object-specific animations (different for each item)
- Environmental reactions (wind, rain, heat effects)
- Physics-based responses (weight affects movement)

ASCII STAGE FORMATTING:
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ SKY LAYER    (weather, time of day)     │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ BACK LAYER   (distant objects)          │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ MID LAYER    (character plane)  ╭╮  ╭╮  │
│                                ╭╯╰──╯╰╮ │
│ ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓│ ⌣  ⌣ │ │
│ GROUND LAYER ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓│   ⌢   │ │
│              ▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓▓╰┬────┬╯ │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ UI LAYER     (dialogue, prompts)        │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

To make this truly Wallace & Gromit-like, you'll want:

1. **TIMING CURVES** - Not just frame counts but easing (slow-in/slow-out)
2. **ANTICIPATION FRAMES** - Before any major movement
3. **FOLLOW-THROUGH** - Ears/accessories lag behind main movement
4. **SQUASH/STRETCH** - Even with ASCII constraints
5. **PERSONALITY QUIRKS** - Unique idle habits, preferred expressions
6. **COMEDIC BEATS** - Pause frames for comic timing

The key is making each character feel alive even when standing still!

This postcard template captures flatboy's dimensional struggles with dry humor - from getting stuck in mail slots to discovering non-existence. The multiple choice format lets people create their own absurd vacation update from the 2D realm!
